\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{I}_{1}(|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}),

and

\displaystyle I_{h2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot(\frac{\nabla_{v}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}f_{R}^{\alpha}-\nabla_{v}f_{R}^{\alpha})\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\sum_{\alpha=A,B}e^{\alpha}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot(\frac{v-\mathbf{u}}{2}f_{R}^{\alpha})f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim(\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}})^{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{\kappa^{2}}\|f^{\alpha}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})f^{A}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\kappa}\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})f^{A}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Considering the decomposition

\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\bigl\langle E_{7}^{\alpha},\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\bigr\rangle_{x,v}=:I_{h3}+I_{h4}+I_{h5},

Lemma
2.5
establishes the boundedness of the following norms:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}F_{i}^{\alpha}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}},\quad\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}\frac{\nabla_{x,v}F_{i}^{\alpha}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}},\quad\|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\|_{p}\lesssim(1+t)^{i-1},\\
&\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}F_{i}^{\alpha}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}},\quad\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}\frac{\nabla_{x,v}F_{i}^{\alpha}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}},\quad for\quad 1<p\leq\infty.\end{split}

Consequently,

\displaystyle I_{h3}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}-\frac{(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}F_{2k-1}^{\alpha}\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx\lesssim(1+t)^{i-1}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle I_{h4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\varepsilon^{i+j-2k+1}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\alpha}_{i}\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+t)^{i-1}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle I_{h5}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k\atop 1\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\varepsilon^{i+j-2k}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}}}\Big[\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}(f^{\alpha}_{i},f^{\beta}_{j})+\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}(f^{\alpha}_{j},f^{\beta}_{i})\Big]\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{2k\leq i+j\leq 4k-2}\varepsilon^{i+j-k+1}(1+t)^{i+j-2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

Hence, insert the above estimates into (
3.6
), and using Lemma
3.1
we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}

\displaystyle\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{c_{1}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{{L^{2}_{x}}}

\displaystyle\,\,\,+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})

\displaystyle\,\,\,+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Choosing
\kappa<\frac{c_{1}}{2}
small enough, the last term on the right-hand side could be absorbed into the
dissipation term, so that (
3.4
) yields for
\gamma=1
.

Next, let us turn to potential
-1\leq\gamma<1
, in this case, the weight function takes the form

e^{\tilde{\kappa}\langle v\rangle^{(3-\gamma)/2}}.

The estimates

\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\bigl|\langle E_{i}^{\alpha},\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\rangle_{x,v}\bigr|,\quad i\in\{1,3,7\},

together with the
I_{h1}
component of

\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\bigl|\langle E_{6}^{\alpha},\theta f_{R}^{\alpha}\rangle_{x,v}\bigr|,

coincide with those in the hard sphere case.
Following analogous arguments yields

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\Big|\left\langle E^{\alpha}_{4},\theta f^{\alpha}_{R}\right\rangle_{x,v}\Big|=

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big|\left\langle\Gamma^{\alpha\beta}(f_{R}^{\alpha},f_{R}^{\beta}),\theta f^{\alpha}_{R}\right\rangle_{x,v}\Big|\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\Big|\left\langle E^{\alpha}_{5},\theta f^{\alpha}_{R}\right\rangle_{x,v}\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{\alpha=A,B}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-\mathbf{u}|f^{\alpha}_{R}\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}{\omega_{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}dv|^{2}dx\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

The terms containing differences are analyzed as follows.

Given
a_{r}=(3-\gamma)^{-1}
, the velocity space
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
is partitioned into

\left\{|v|<\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a_{r}}}\right\}\,\,\text{and}\,\,\left\{|v|\geq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a_{r}}}\right\},

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\Big|\left\langle E^{\alpha}_{2},e^{\alpha}\theta f^{\alpha}_{R}\right\rangle_{x,v}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|(n^{A},n^{B},\mathbf{u},\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{f}_{R}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\geq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|(n^{A},n^{B},\mathbf{u},\theta)\|_{W_{x}^{1,\infty}}\,\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{4}}\mathbf{f}_{R}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a}}}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{3-\gamma}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\geq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a}}}\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3}{4}}\mathcal{P}\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\||v|^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a}}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}dx\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}|^{2}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\varepsilon^{a}}}dv

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa^{3-\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Moreover, by (
1.8
) and Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, the potential function
\phi_{i}
satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}&=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Delta\phi_{i}\phi_{i}\,dx=-\sum_{\alpha=A,B}e^{\alpha}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}F_{i}^{\alpha}\phi_{i}\,dv\,dx\\
&\lesssim\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|F_{i}^{\alpha}|}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}|\phi_{i}|\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}\,dv\,dx\lesssim|\phi_{i}|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\frac{F_{i}^{\alpha}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}L^{\frac{6}{5}}_{x}}\lesssim(1+t)^{i-1}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{2}_{x}},\end{split}

which implies
|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim(1+t)^{i-1}
, then the
I_{32}
part of
\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\Big|\left\langle E^{\alpha}_{6},\theta f^{\alpha}_{R}\right\rangle_{x,v}\Big|
is bounded by

\displaystyle I_{h2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\sum_{\alpha=A,B}e^{\alpha}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot(\frac{v-\mathbf{u}}{2}f_{R}^{\alpha})f_{R}^{\alpha}\,dv\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big(\iint_{|v|\geq\frac{1}{\frac{\kappa}{1-\gamma}}}+\iint_{|v|\leq\frac{1}{\frac{\kappa}{1-\gamma}}}\Big)|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{f}_{R}^{2}dv\,dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\Big(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\omega_{\gamma}\mathbf{f}_{R}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\geq\frac{1}{\frac{\kappa}{1-\gamma}}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}|^{2}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq\frac{1}{\frac{\kappa}{1-\gamma}}}dv\,dx\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}(1+t)^{i-1}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa^{1-\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa^{1-\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Consequently, whenever
-1\leq\gamma<1
, it follows that:

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}&\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{c_{0}}{2\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}\\
&\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{{L^{2}_{x}}}\\
&\,\,\,+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}.\end{split}

(3.8)

∎

Remark 3.4
.

For the potential range
-3<\gamma<-1
, the weight function
\omega_{\gamma}
should be replaced by
\omega_{-1}
. The remaining procedure follows the case of
-1\leq\gamma<1
, with
\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}
replaced by
\mathbf{S}_{-1}
.

4.
The
W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}
estimate of the remainder term for hard sphere

To establish energy closure in the
L^{2}
estimates (
3.4
), weighted
L^{\infty}
estimates are required for either
\mathbf{h}
or
\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}
, which is obtained through Duhamel’s principle along the VPB system trajectory. Unlike the compressible Euler limit
[
20
]
, the interaction between
L^{2}
and
L^{\infty}
norms fails to achieve complete energy closure, owing to the self-consistent electric field
\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi\cdot\nabla_{v}F^{\alpha}
. This analytical challenge necessitates additional estimation of the derivatives’
L^{\infty}
norms.
The subsequent analysis proceeds as follows: Section 4 presents essential lemmas and proves the hard sphere case (
\gamma=1
), while Section 5 addresses the range
-1\leq\gamma<1
. For
-3<\gamma<-1
, Section 6 examines the corresponding reduction in the validity time.

First, recalling the global Maxwellian defined in (
1.4
),
for the vector function
\mathbf{g}=(g^{A},g^{B})^{T}
, we introduce
the linear operator

\mathbf{L}_{M}=(\mathbf{L}_{M}^{A},\mathbf{L}_{M}^{B})^{T}

as following

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{L}^{\alpha}_{M}\mathbf{g}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\left[Q^{\alpha\beta}(\mu^{\alpha},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}g^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}g^{\alpha},\mu^{\beta})\right].\end{split}

It can be decomposed to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\begin{array}[]{lll}\mathbf{L}_{M}^{A}\mathbf{g}\\
\mathbf{L}_{M}^{B}\mathbf{g}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{lll}\upsilon^{A}g^{A}+K_{M}^{A}\mathbf{g}\\
\upsilon^{B}g^{B}+K_{M}^{B}\mathbf{g}\end{array}\right),\end{split}

where

\displaystyle\upsilon^{\alpha}=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}B^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})d\sigma dv_{*},\quad K_{M}^{\alpha}\mathbf{g}=K_{M,1}^{\alpha}\mathbf{g}+K_{M,2}^{\alpha}\mathbf{g},

\displaystyle K_{M,1}^{\alpha}\mathbf{g}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}B^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\mu^{\alpha}(v)\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}g^{\beta}(v_{*})d\sigma dv_{*},

\displaystyle K_{M,2}^{\alpha}\mathbf{g}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}B^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\Big[\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}g^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})

(4.1)

\displaystyle\hskip 193.47882pt+\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\,g^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\Big]d\sigma dv_{*}.

Moreover, there exists a constant
C
such that

\displaystyle C^{-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\leq\upsilon^{\alpha}\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma},\qquad{\rm for}\qquad\alpha=A,B.

A smooth cutoff function
\chi_{\delta}
with
0\leq\chi_{\delta}\leq 1
is introduced to regularize the singular part of the operators
K^{\alpha\beta}_{M}
. That is for any
\delta>0
, the function
\chi_{\delta}(s)
satisfies:

\chi_{\delta}(s)\equiv 1\quad\textnormal{for}\,s\leq\delta,\quad{\rm and}\quad\chi_{\delta}(s)\equiv 0,\quad\textnormal{for}\,s\geq 2\delta.

(4.2)

Finally, let
B^{\alpha\beta}_{\chi}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)=:B^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\chi_{\delta}(|v-v_{*}|)
, and we define the following operators

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,1}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}B_{\chi}^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\mu^{\alpha}(v)\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}g^{\beta}(v_{*})d\sigma dv_{*},\\
&K_{M,2}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}B_{\chi}^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\Big[\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}g^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 193.47882pt+\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\,g^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\Big]d\sigma dv_{*},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,1}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}=(K_{M,1}^{\alpha}-K_{M,1}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g},\qquad K_{M,2}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}=(K_{M,2}^{\alpha}-K_{M,2}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g},\\
&K_{M}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}=K_{M,1}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}+K_{M,2}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g},\qquad K_{M}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}=K_{M,1}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}+K_{M,2}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}.\end{split}

Using the weight function
w_{\gamma}(v)
defined in
(
1.14
), we denote

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,w}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{g})=wK_{M}^{\alpha}(\frac{\mathbf{g}}{w}),\quad K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}(\mathbf{g})=wK_{M}^{\alpha,\chi}(\frac{\mathbf{g}}{w}),\quad K_{M,w}^{\alpha,c}(\mathbf{g})=wK_{M}^{\alpha,c}(\frac{\mathbf{g}}{w}),\\
&K_{M,i,w}^{\alpha,c}(\mathbf{g})=wK_{M,i}^{\alpha,c}(\frac{\mathbf{g}}{w}),\quad K_{M,i,w}^{\alpha,\chi}(\mathbf{g})=wK_{M,i}^{\alpha,\chi}(\frac{\mathbf{g}}{w}),\quad\quad\quad i=1,2.\end{split}

(4.3)

Lemma 4.1
.

For the weight function
w_{\gamma}(v)
defined in (
1.14
),
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and sufficiently small
\delta>0
, there exist constants
C>1
(large) and
c>0
(small) such that the singular operator
K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}
satisfies the estimate:

\displaystyle|K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}(v)|+|D_{x}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|+|D_{v}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C{\delta}^{3+\gamma}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-c|v|^{2}}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},

(4.4)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}(D_{x}K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}:=D_{x}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g})-K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{x}\mathbf{g}),\\
(D_{v}K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}:=D_{v}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g})-K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{v}\mathbf{g}).\end{split}

(4.5)

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we take the weight function
w(v)=\omega_{\gamma}
to give the proof, since it increases the fastest with respect to the velocity variable
v
. By (
1.4
), (c.f
[
20
]
), we have

\displaystyle c_{1}\mu_{M}^{\alpha}\leq\mu^{\alpha}\leq c_{2}(\mu_{M}^{\alpha})^{\tilde{q}},\quad{\rm for\,\,some}\quad 1>\tilde{q}>\frac{\max\{m^{A},m^{B}\}}{m^{A}+m^{B}}\geq\frac{1}{2}.

From the conservation law
m^{\alpha}|v^{\prime}|^{2}+m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}=m^{\alpha}|v|^{2}+m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}
for
\alpha,\beta\in\{A,B\}
, one can get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\leq&c_{2}[\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})]^{\tilde{q}}\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\leq C[\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})]^{\tilde{q}-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})},\\
\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\leq&c_{2}[\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})]^{\tilde{q}}\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\leq C[\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})]^{\tilde{q}-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})},\\
\mu^{\alpha}(v)\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\leq&c_{2}[\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)]^{\tilde{q}-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}.\end{split}

Since
|v-v_{*}|\leq 2\delta<2
, then one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})&=(2\pi\theta_{M})^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}}{2\theta_{M}}}\leq(2\pi\theta_{M})^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{m^{\beta}\{|v|^{2}-|v-v_{*}|^{2}\}}{2\theta_{M}}}\leq C\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v),\\
\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}&=e^{\tilde{\kappa}[\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}-\left\langle v_{*}\right\rangle^{2}]}\leq C,\qquad\{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v)\}^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}.\end{split}

Thus, there exist a small constant
c>0
, such that for all
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}|K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C{\delta}^{3+\gamma}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-c|v|^{2}}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

Moreover, it can be directly calculated that

|(D_{x}K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}|K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}(v)|,\quad|(D_{v}K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle|K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{g}(v)|,

where we have used the fact that
\left\langle v_{*}\right\rangle^{2}\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}
. Since
\{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v)\}^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|+|D_{v}(K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C{\delta}^{2+\gamma}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-c|v|^{2}}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

∎

Remark 4.2
.

The term
|(D_{v}K_{M,w}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{g}(v)|
involves differentiating the collision kernel, yielding
\frac{d}{dv}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}=\gamma|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma-1}
for
|v-v_{*}|\leq 2\delta
. This derivative introduces a stronger singularity near
v=v_{*}
, and seems to make the expression not integrable when
-3<\gamma\leq-2
. However, we can remove the singularity through ‌a change of variable “
v+v_{*}\rightarrow v_{*}
”. Consequently, the validity range of the conclusion remains
-3<\gamma\leq 1
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under the hypotheses of Lemma
4.1
and using the notation from (
4.3
), the regular component
K_{M,w}^{\alpha,c}
admits the decomposition:

K_{M,w}^{\alpha,c}=K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}+K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c}.

Both parts
K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,\chi}
and
K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,\chi}
can be represented as integral operators of the form:

\displaystyle\begin{split}K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}(v,v_{*})\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}g^{\beta}(v_{*})dv_{*},\end{split}

(4.6)

\displaystyle\begin{split}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)&=K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(1)}\mathbf{g}(v)+K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(2)}\mathbf{g}(v)\\
&=\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\parallel})}g^{\beta}(v+u_{\parallel})du_{\parallel}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(2)}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\perp}+u_{\parallel})}g^{\beta}(v+u_{\perp}+u_{\parallel})du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}\Big].\end{split}

(4.7)

Here, we adopt the shorthand
w=w_{\gamma}
. There exists a small constant
c>0
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}|k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}(v,v_{*})|&\leq C_{\delta}\,e^{-c\{|v|^{2}+|v_{*}|^{2}\}},\\
\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\parallel})}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})|&\leq C_{\delta}\frac{(1+|v|+|u_{\parallel}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|u_{\parallel}|}\,e^{-c\{|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\parallel}|^{2}\}}\\
\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\perp}+u_{\parallel})}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(2)}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})|&\leq C_{\delta}\,e^{-c[|v|^{2}+|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}]},\end{split}

(4.8)

where

v=v_{\parallel}+v_{\perp},\quad|v|^{2}=|v_{\parallel}|^{2}+|v_{\perp}|^{2},\quad|u|^{2}\thicksim(|u_{\parallel}|^{2}+|u_{\perp}|^{2}).

These kernel operators exhibit distinct decay properties:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})|\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\parallel})}du_{\parallel}\leq C_{\delta}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2},\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(2)}(v,u_{\perp},u_{\parallel})|\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\perp}+u_{\parallel})}du_{\perp}du_{\parallel}\leq C_{\delta}\,e^{-c|v|^{2}}.\end{split}

(4.9)

The preceding analysis yields the following estimates:

\displaystyle|K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)|+|(D_{x}K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|+|(D_{v}K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C_{\delta}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{c}{2}|v|^{2}}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},

(4.10)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}|K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)|+|(D_{x}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|&\leq C_{\delta}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},\\
|(D_{v}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|&\leq C_{\delta}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-1}|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

(4.11)

Remark 4.4
.

The linear operator
K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c}
for mixed gases
(m^{A}\neq m^{B})
consists of two distinct components:

K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c}=\underbrace{K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(1)}}_{\text{Partial decay part}}+\underbrace{K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(2)}}_{\text{Total decay part}}

where the second term is an additional part compared to the Boltzmann equation for single-species particles.‌ The first term can be reformulated through a variable substitution as

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,u_{\parallel})\frac{w(v)}{w(v+u_{\parallel})}g^{\beta}(v+u_{\parallel})du_{\parallel}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\tilde{k}_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,v_{*})\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}g^{\beta}(v_{*})dv_{*},

and satisfies the estimate identical to those for single-species particles:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}|\tilde{k}_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}(v,v_{*})|\leq C_{\delta}\frac{(1+|v|+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|v-v_{*}|}\,e^{-c(|v-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|v-v_{*}|^{2}})}.\end{split}

(4.12)

Proof.

The proof of (
4.6
)-(
4.9
) as well as
Remark
4.4
can be found in my recent work

[
40
]
. We only prove (
4.10
) and (
4.11
)
in the following. First, a direct calculation leads to

|(D_{x}K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}|K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)|,\quad|(D_{v}K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle|K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)|.

The inequality estimation derived from (
4.8
) demonstrates:

\begin{split}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}|K_{M,1,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq&C\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}\,e^{-c\{|v|^{2}+|v_{*}|^{2}\}}dv_{*}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\\
\leq&C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\,e^{-\frac{3c}{4}\{|v|^{2}+|v_{*}|^{2}\}}dv_{*}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}e^{-\frac{c}{2}|v|^{2}}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

Next, considering the definition in (
4.3
), we have

\begin{split}&|(D_{x}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\Big[\left\langle v^{\prime}\right\rangle^{2}\mu^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt+\left\langle v_{*}^{\prime}\right\rangle^{2}\mu^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\Big]d\sigma dv_{*}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

From (
4
) with
\tilde{q}-\frac{1}{2}=2c_{s}>0
and sufficiently small
\kappa_{0}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(D_{x}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\leq C\sum_{\beta=A,B}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\Big[\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}[\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})]^{c_{s}}\\
&\hskip 31.2982pt+\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}[\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})]^{c_{s}}\Big]d\sigma dv_{*}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

(4.13)

By utilizing Remark
4.4
, it can be derived that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&|(D_{x}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(1+|v|+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|v-v_{*}|}\,\,e^{-2c\{|v-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|v-v_{*}|^{2}}\}}\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})}dv_{*}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-2}|\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

Since
|\frac{dv^{\prime}}{dv}|+|\frac{dv_{*}^{\prime}}{dv}|\leq C
, from the chain rule of differentiation, and the conservation law, we follow the established reasoning‌ through a ‌parallel process‌ while ‌invoking Remark
4.4
, the result emerges that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(D_{v}K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{g}(v)|\lesssim&\left\langle v\right\rangle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{w(v)(1+|v|+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma-1}}{w(v_{*})|v-v_{*}|}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times e^{-2c\{|v-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||v|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|v-v_{*}|^{2}}\}}dv_{*}|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\\
\leq&C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-1}|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{g}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

∎

Remark 4.5
.

The constant
C>0
and the small constant
c>0
depend on the particle masses
m^{A}

and
m^{B}

as well as the macroscopic quantities of the fluid
(n^{A},n^{B},\mathbf{u},\theta)
, which are not only bounded but also possess positive lower bounds.

Remark 4.6
.

For the weight function
\omega_{\gamma}=e^{\tilde{\kappa}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}},\tilde{\kappa}=\kappa_{0}[1+(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{2k-1}}]
. When
\frac{3-\gamma}{2}=2
, the parameter
\kappa_{0}

should be sufficiently small to ensure the inequalities (
4.10
) and (
4.11
).

The following lemma is used in the
W_{x,v}^{1,\infty}
estimates for nonlinear terms.

Lemma 4.7
.

We abbreviate
w_{\gamma}(v)
in (
1.14
) to
w
.
For\,\,any~\alpha,\beta\in\{A,B\}
and
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})(v)\right|\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}|g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

(4.14)

Furthermore, the following estimate holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|D_{x}\Big[\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})\Big](v)\right|\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\Big(|D_{x}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+|D_{x}g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\Big),\end{split}

(4.15)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|D_{v}\Big[\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})\Big](v)\right|\lesssim&\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\Big(|D_{v}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+|D_{v}g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\Big)\\
&+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}|\left\langle v\right\rangle g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|\left\langle v\right\rangle g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

(4.16)

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we take the weight function
w_{\gamma}(v)=\omega_{\gamma}
to give the proof, since it increases the fastest with respect to the velocity variable
v
. By the conservation of energy
m^{\alpha}|v^{\prime}|^{2}+m^{\beta}|v_{*}^{\prime}|^{2}=m^{\alpha}|v|^{2}+m^{\beta}|v_{*}|^{2}
for
\alpha,\beta\in\{A,B\}
, we obtain

\displaystyle w(v)\leq Cw(v_{*}^{\prime})w(v^{\prime})\,,\,\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}=\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}.

For the gain term, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\frac{w(v)}{\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v)}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\,\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}\frac{g^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}{w(v^{\prime})}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}\frac{g^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}{w(v_{*}^{\prime})}d\sigma dv_{*}\right|\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\leq C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}\,|w(v^{\prime})w(v_{*}^{\prime})\frac{g^{\alpha}(v^{\prime})}{w(v^{\prime})}\frac{g^{\beta}(v_{*}^{\prime})}{w(v_{*}^{\prime})}|\,dv_{*}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\leq C|g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}|wg^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}dv_{*}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim(1+|v|)^{\gamma}|wg_{1}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|wg_{2}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

Regarding the loss term, the following holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|w(v)g^{\alpha}(v)\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}b^{\alpha\beta}(\theta)\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}g^{\beta}(v_{*})d\sigma dv_{*}\right|\\
&\leq|w(v)g^{\alpha}(v)|\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\sqrt{\mu_{M}^{\beta}(v_{*})}dv_{*}\right|\lesssim(1+|v|)^{\gamma}\,|wg^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|wg^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},\end{split}

which yields

\displaystyle\left|\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})(v)\right|\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}|g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Since
w
and
\mu_{M}^{\alpha}
are the functions of
t,v
, through a direct calculation, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}D_{x}\Big[\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})\Big]\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}(|D_{x}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+|D_{x}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}).\end{split}

Moreover, the following is derived:

\displaystyle\left|D_{v}\Big[\frac{w}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\,Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{g^{\alpha}}{w},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{g^{\beta}}{w})\Big]\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}(|D_{v}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+|D_{v}g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}})+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}|\left\langle v\right\rangle g^{\alpha}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,|\left\langle v\right\rangle g^{\beta}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

In conclusion, (
4.15
),(
4.16
) hold for all
\alpha,\beta\in\{A,B\}
. This completes the proof of Lemma
4.7
.
∎

For
\gamma=1
, we derive the equations for
\mathbf{h}=(h^{A},h^{B})^{T}
from (
1.9
). That is,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{t}h^{\alpha}&+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\alpha}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}+\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}h^{\alpha}=\sum_{j=1}^{6}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{j}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h},\end{split}

(4.17)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{1}=\varepsilon^{k-1}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}Q^{\alpha\beta}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\alpha},\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\beta}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2}^{\alpha}=\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\alpha},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big],

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}=-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})h^{\alpha},

(4.18)

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4}=-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}=-\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[\varepsilon^{k-1}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})F^{\alpha}_{2k-1}+\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}\Big],

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6}=\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big\{\sum_{\beta=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k\atop 1\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k-1}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},F_{j}^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{j}^{\alpha},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big]\Big\}.

The characteristic curve of (
4.17
) passing through
(t,x,v)
is defined as the solution of the equations

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{d}{d\tau}X^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v),\quad X^{\alpha}(t;t,x,v)=x,\\
&\frac{d}{d\tau}V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)),\quad V^{\alpha}(t;t,x,v)=v.\end{split}

(4.19)

One can verify that
\frac{d}{d\tau}h^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v))=\partial_{t}h^{\alpha}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\alpha}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}
.

Lemma 4.8
.

Recall the weighted remainder
\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}
defined in (
1.16
). For
0\leq T_{1}\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
, assuming that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{\tau\in[0,T_{1}]}\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}(t)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\leq\sqrt{\varepsilon}.\end{split}

(4.20)

Consider the vectors
X^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=(X_{1}^{\alpha},X_{2}^{\alpha},X_{3}^{\alpha})
,
V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=(V_{1}^{\alpha},V_{2}^{\alpha},V_{3}^{\alpha})
, with spatial coordinates
x=(x_{1},x_{2},x_{3})
and velocity coordinates
v=(v_{1},v_{2},v_{3})
. For each
1\leq i,j\leq 3
, the assumption (
4.20
) implies

\begin{split}&\sup_{\tau\in[0,T_{1}]}\Big(\|\frac{\partial X_{i}^{\alpha}}{\partial v_{j}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\partial X_{i}^{\alpha}}{\partial x_{j}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\partial V_{i}^{\alpha}}{\partial v_{j}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\|\frac{\partial V_{i}^{\alpha}}{\partial x_{j}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\Big)\\
&\leq C(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})+C\varepsilon^{k}\|R^{\alpha}_{\gamma}(t)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\leq C.\end{split}

(4.21)

Moreover, there exists a sufficiently small
T_{0}\in[0,T_{1}]
such that for all
0\leq\tau\leq t\leq T_{0}
,
N\geq 1
and
x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{|t-\tau|^{3}}{2}\leq\Big|\rm{det}\Big(\frac{\partial X(\tau)}{\partial v}\Big)\Big|\leq 2|t-\tau|^{3},\qquad|\partial_{v}X(\tau)|\leq 2|t-\tau|,\quad\frac{1}{2}\leq\Big|\rm{det}\Big(\frac{\partial V(\tau)}{\partial v}\Big)\Big|\leq 2,\\
&\frac{1}{2}\leq\Big|\rm{det}\Big(\frac{\partial X(\tau)}{\partial x}\Big)\Big|\leq 2,\qquad\sup_{|v|\leq N}\Big[\int_{|x-x_{0}|\leq CN}(|\partial_{x,v}X(\tau)|^{2}+|\partial_{v,v}X(\tau)|^{2})dx\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{N}.\end{split}

(4.22)

As the ratio
\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}
remains bounded and the time interval
T_{0}\in[0,T_{1}]
is sufficiently small, the argument for Lemma
4.8
follows directly from the proof of Lemma 4.1 in
[
15
]
, hence the details are omitted.

\mathbf{The\,\,W_{x,v}^{1,\infty}\,\,estimates\,\,for\,\,the\,\,remainder\,\,term\,\,is\,\,in\,\,the\,\,following\,\,proposition}.

Proposition 4.9
.

Let
(n^{A},n^{B},\mathbf{u},\theta)\in C([0,\tau];H^{s_{0}}(\mathbb{{R}}^{3}))\cap C^{1}([0,\tau];H^{s_{0}-1}(\mathbb{{R}}^{3}))
be the solution of (
2.2
), and
(
2.3
)
.
\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}
is the weighted remainder term defined in (
1.17
).
Then there exist
\varepsilon_{0}>0
such that for all
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,T_{L}]}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}})\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{R}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{s\in[0,T_{L}]}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{3}{2}},

(4.23)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{s\in[0,T_{L}]}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{R}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}})\\
\lesssim&\quad\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{R}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{s\in[0,T_{L}]}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{R}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{3}{2}}.\end{split}

(4.24)

\mathbf{Proof\,\,for\,\,hard\,\,sphere\,\,case}(\gamma=1)
.

By Duhamel’s
Principle, the equation (
4.17
) gives the expression of
h^{\alpha}
along the characteristics (
4.19
) as

\displaystyle\begin{split}h^{\alpha}(t,x,v)=&\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)h^{\alpha}(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)\sum_{i=1}^{6}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{j}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.\end{split}

(4.25)

Introduce the notation:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)=:\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right).

Since
\frac{1}{C}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\leq\upsilon^{\alpha}(t,x,v)\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}
, there exists a small constant
\nu_{o}
such that

\inf_{t,x,v}\{\upsilon^{\alpha}(t,x,v)\}\geq 2\nu_{o}>0.

(4.26)

Moreover, when
|v|\leq 1
, for sufficiently small
T_{0}>0
, the relation (
4.21
) implies

\sup_{s\in[0,T_{0}]}|V^{\alpha}(s)|\leq C.

Then, for
0\leq s\leq t\leq T_{0}
, the following estimate holds

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{\varepsilon}}ds\right|\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle(t-s)}{C\varepsilon}\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle ds\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}.

When
|v|>1
and
T_{0}>0
is sufficiently small, the characteristic velocity satisfies the following uniform bounds

\frac{|v|}{2}\leq|V^{\alpha}(s)|\leq 2|v|.

These velocity bounds lead to the refined estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{\varepsilon}}ds\right|\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle(t-s)}{C\varepsilon}\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle ds\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}.

(4.27)

4.1.
L^{\infty}
estimates

In the following, we give the estimates in right hand side of (
4.25
) line to line.
First, the initial data contribution yields the exponential decay estimate

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)h^{\alpha}(0,\,X^{\alpha}(0),\,V^{\alpha}(0))\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Then, from Lemma
4.7
(
\gamma=1
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{H}_{1}^{\alpha}|=&\varepsilon^{k-1}\left|\sum_{\beta=A,B}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{h^{\alpha}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{h^{\beta}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})\right|\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\,\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

(4.28)

which together with (
4.27
) leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{1}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim&\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{\varepsilon}}\left\langle v\right\rangle ds[\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]^{2}\\
\lesssim&\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}.\end{split}

Next, the construction of
\mathbf{F}_{i}
in Lemma
2.5
gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{H}_{2}^{\alpha}|\lesssim&\,\left\langle v\right\rangle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big(\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}F_{i}^{\beta}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,\Big\|h^{\alpha}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\Big\|h^{\beta}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}F_{i}^{\alpha}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\\
\lesssim&\,\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{1}\,\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For this reason, we see

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{2}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim&\,\mathcal{I}_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle v\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})ds\\
\lesssim&\mathcal{I}_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle v\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}ds\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
\lesssim&\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Moreover, the estimates from
\eqref{ORDERZONG}_{1}
and
\eqref{ORDERZONG}_{3}
combine to produce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\Delta\phi_{0}=&\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\alpha}F^{\alpha}_{0}dv-\bar{n}_{e},\quad\Delta\phi_{i}=\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\alpha}F^{\alpha}_{i}dv,\qquad i\geq 1,\\
\Delta\phi_{R}=&\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\alpha}\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\alpha}\,dv,\\
\Delta\phi^{\varepsilon}=&\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\alpha}F^{\alpha}_{i}dv+\varepsilon^{k}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{\alpha}\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\alpha}\,dv\Big]-\bar{n}_{e}.\end{split}

(4.29)

Then for any
0<\alpha_{0}<1
, it holds that

|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{C^{1,\alpha_{0}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{h}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}},\qquad|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|_{C^{1,\alpha_{0}}_{x}}\lesssim 1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}\leq C.

(4.30)

Together with

\frac{\langle v\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\langle v\rangle^{l}})\lesssim\langle v\rangle

and

\frac{\langle v\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha})\lesssim(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\langle v\rangle,

we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}|=

\displaystyle|\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})h^{\alpha}|\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

(4.31)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4}|=

\displaystyle|\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha})|\lesssim(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Consequently, one obtains

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}+\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle v\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})ds

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Finally, the estimate of
\mathbf{F}_{i}
in Lemma
2.5
further implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}|\lesssim&\left\langle v\right\rangle\Big[\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l-1}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})F^{\alpha}_{2k-1}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\hskip 99.58464pt+\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big\|\frac{\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big]\\
\lesssim&\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{2},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6}|=&\Big|\sum_{\beta=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k\atop 1\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k-1}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},F_{j}^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{j}^{\alpha},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big]\Big|\\
\lesssim&\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}\left\langle v\right\rangle\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim&\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

Combining these estimates yields the integral bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}+\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle v\right\rangle ds\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

(4.32)

By Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim{\delta}^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

The analysis focuses primarily on the final term in (
4.25
)’s right-hand side. Lemma
4.3
establishes that
K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(2)}
demonstrates superior decay properties compared to
K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(1)}
. Consequently, the decay estimates require consideration only for
K_{M,2,w}^{\alpha,c,(1)}
, that is

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}+k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}+k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(2)}](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\frac{\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle^{l}}{\left\langle v_{*}\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.\end{split}

(4.33)

For brevity, we denote:

k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}:=[k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}+k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(1)}+k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta(2)}]\frac{w(v)}{w(v_{*})},

(4.34)

where
w(v)=\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}
for
\gamma=1
. The “double characteristic line” is defined as

\Big[X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\Big]=\Big[X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),v_{*}),V^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),v_{*})\Big],

(4.35)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\upsilon^{\beta}(\tau,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(\tau),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(\tau))d\tau\right).\end{split}

h^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})
could be formulated as

\displaystyle\begin{split}h^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})=&\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,0)h^{\beta}(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))\\
&+\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}.\end{split}

(4.36)

Then, substituting (
4.36
) into (
4.33
) yields

\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\leq\sum_{i=0}^{2}\mathcal{J}_{i}(t)+\mathcal{O}^{\alpha}(t),

(4.37)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{J}_{0}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,0)h^{\beta}(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))dv_{*}ds\\
&\hskip 22.76228pt\lesssim\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\end{split}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{J}_{1}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 128.0374pt\times\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j}(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
&\hskip 22.76228pt\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}(\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+({1+\mathcal{I}_{1}})\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\mathcal{I}_{2}\varepsilon^{k},\end{split}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{J}_{2}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 99.58464pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\lesssim\delta^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

The last term in (
4.37
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{O}^{\alpha}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta,c}\mathbf{h})(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
\leq&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|h^{\beta^{\prime}}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})dv_{**}ds_{1}dv_{*}ds.\end{split}

From (
4.19
) and (
4.21
), for sufficiently large
N>0
and small
T_{0}
, the following holds:

\sup_{0\leq s,t\leq T_{0}}|V^{\alpha}(s)-v|\leq\frac{N}{2},\quad\alpha\in\{A,B\}.

(4.38)

By Lemma
4.3
(
\gamma=1
), it yields that

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|dv_{**}\,dv_{*}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\frac{C\left\langle V(s)\right\rangle}{1+|V(s)|}\times\left\langle V_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle.\end{split}

We estimate
\mathcal{O}^{\alpha}
in the following cases.

Case 1
. For
|v|\geq N
. From (
4.38
) we get
|V^{\alpha}(s)|\geq\frac{N}{2}
, which leads to

\begin{split}&\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|dv_{**}\,dv_{*}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\frac{C}{N}\times\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle\times\left\langle V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle.\end{split}

Thus, one obtains the following bound

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}^{\alpha}(t)\mathbf{1}_{|v|\geq N}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}N}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})\left\langle V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s_{1}}{2\varepsilon}}ds_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\sup_{0\leq s_{1}\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s_{1}}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\,\,ds

(4.39)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{N}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Case 2
. For either
|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\geq 2N
or
|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\geq 3N
. We note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|\geq|v_{*}-v|-|V^{\alpha}(s)-v|\geq|v_{*}|-|v|-|V^{\alpha}(s)-v|,\\
|V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})-v_{**}|\geq|v_{**}-v_{*}|-|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})-v_{*}|\geq|v_{**}|-|v_{*}|-|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})-v_{*}|.\end{split}

For sufficiently small
0<\eta\leq c
, either
|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|\geq\frac{N}{2}
or
|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})-v_{**}|\geq\frac{N}{2}
holds. Consequently, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})&\leq e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|^{2}},\\
k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})&\leq e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})-v_{**}|^{2}}.\end{split}

Further, from Lemma
4.3
, since
0\leq\eta\leq c
is sufficiently small, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|^{2}}dv_{*}&\leq C\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle,\\
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})-v_{**}|^{2}}dv_{**}&\leq C\left\langle V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle.\end{split}

This leads to the following bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\geq 2N}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\int_{|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\geq 3N}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|h^{\beta^{\prime}}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})dv_{**}ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
&\leq\frac{C_{\eta}}{\varepsilon^{2}}e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})\left\langle V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s_{1}}{2\varepsilon}}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s_{1}}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s_{1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,ds_{1}\,ds\\
&\leq C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Case 3
. If
|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|>2N
, it is also included in Case 2; while if
|v_{**}|>3N
, either
|v_{*}|\leq 2N
or
|v_{*}|\geq 2N
is also included in Case 2.
The remaining case is that
|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N
, that is,
v,v_{*},v_{**}
are all bounded. Then we
assume that
s-s_{1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda
and
s-s_{1}\geq\varepsilon\lambda
where the
\lambda
is small enough.

Case 3a
. In this case,
|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N
and
s-s_{1}\leq\varepsilon\lambda
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{s-\lambda\varepsilon}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|h^{\beta^{\prime}}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})dv_{**}ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\Big|\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{s-\lambda\varepsilon}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\left\langle V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s_{1}}{2\varepsilon}}\,ds_{1}\,ds\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\leq C\lambda e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Case 3b
. In the case
|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N,
and
s-s_{1}\geq\varepsilon\lambda
where
\lambda
is small enough. It could be bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{|v^{*}|\leq 2N,|v^{**}|\leq 3N}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 68.28644pt\times k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})ds_{1}dv_{**}dv_{*}ds.\end{split}

(4.40)

Moreover, there exists some smooth operators
k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha},v^{*}),k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1},v_{**})\,\alpha,\beta\in\{A,B\}
with compact support, such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{|V^{\alpha}(s)|\leq 3N,|v_{*}|\leq 3N}|k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}-k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v^{*})dv_{*}\leq&\frac{1}{N},\\
\int_{V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})|\leq 3N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N}|k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}-k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})dv_{**}\leq&\frac{1}{N}.\end{split}

(4.41)

Then, we split
k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})
as

\displaystyle\begin{split}&k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\\
=&\Big[k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})-k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\Big]\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&+\Big[k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})-k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\Big]\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\\
&+k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**}).\end{split}

(4.42)

The term can be bounded by combining estimates from (
4.40
), (
4.41
), and (
4.42
). That is

\displaystyle\frac{C}{N\varepsilon^{2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{|v_{*}|\leq 2N}|k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})|dv_{*}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})ds_{1}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{|v_{**}|\leq 3N}|k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})ds_{1}dv_{**}ds\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 256.0748pt\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

(4.43)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{|v^{*}|\leq 2N,|v^{**}|\leq 3N}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}_{M,N}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|ds_{1}dv_{**}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{N}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{|v^{*}|\leq 2N,|v^{**}|\leq 3N}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}_{M,N}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})

\displaystyle\hskip 221.93158pt\times|\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|ds_{1}dv_{**}dv_{*}ds.

(4.44)

Let
\zeta_{\alpha\beta}=X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})=X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),v_{*})
.
By Lemma
4.8
, it follows that

|\zeta_{\beta\alpha}-X^{\alpha}(s)|=|X^{\beta,\alpha}_{1}(s_{1})-X^{\alpha}(s)|\leq C(s-s_{1}).

For
s-s_{1}\geq\lambda\varepsilon
, the derivative bound holds:

\left|\frac{d\zeta_{\beta\alpha}}{dv_{*}}\right|\geq\frac{\lambda^{3}\varepsilon^{3}}{2}.

Since

\Big|k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}_{M,N}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\Big|\leq C_{N},

integrating
v_{*}
over the domain
|v_{*}|\leq 2N
implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{|v_{*}|\leq 2N}|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|dv_{*}\leq C_{N}\Big(\int_{|v_{*}|\leq 2N}|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|^{2}dv_{*}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\frac{C_{N}}{\lambda^{\frac{3}{2}}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(\int_{|\zeta_{\alpha\beta}-X^{\alpha}(s)|\leq C(s-s_{1})N}|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})|^{2}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\frac{C_{N}}{\lambda^{\frac{3}{2}}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})|^{2}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Then the last term in (
4.44
) is further controlled by

\displaystyle\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{7/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{|v_{**}|\leq 3N}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}}|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})|^{2}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv_{**}ds_{1}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{7/2}}\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\kappa}e^{-\frac{\nu(N)(t-s)}{\varepsilon}}e^{-\frac{\nu(2N)(s-s_{1})}{\varepsilon}}\|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds_{1}ds.

For
|v|\leq N
,
|v_{*}|\leq 2N
, and
|v_{**}|\leq 3N
,

|h^{\beta^{\prime}}|=w\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta^{\prime}}}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta^{\prime}}_{M}}}|f^{\beta^{\prime}}_{R}|\leq C_{N}|f^{\beta^{\prime}}_{R}|

implies

\begin{split}&\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{7/2}}\int_{0}^{T_{0}}\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}e^{-\frac{\nu(N)(t-s)}{\varepsilon}}e^{-\frac{\nu(2N)(s-s_{1})}{\varepsilon}}\|h^{\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds_{1}ds\\
&\leq\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{7/2}}\int_{0}^{T_{0}}\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}e^{-\frac{\nu(N)(t-s)}{\varepsilon}}e^{-\frac{\nu(2N)(s-s_{1})}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s_{1}\leq s}\|f^{\beta^{\prime}}_{R}(s_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}ds_{1}ds\\
&\leq\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3/2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Hence, the preceding arguments establish that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}&(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|h^{\alpha}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(1+\frac{s}{\varepsilon})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}]\|\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1}(T_{0}))\varepsilon+C\lambda+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+\delta^{3+1}]\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}(T_{0})e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

(4.45)

For small
\delta>0
, we choose
\lambda
small and
N
sufficiently large such that

C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1}(T_{0}))\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+\delta^{4}<\frac{1}{2}.

Noticing that
(1+\frac{s}{\varepsilon})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}
,
\mathcal{I}_{1}
and
\mathcal{I}_{2}
are uniformly bounded, with assumption (
4.20
), one obtains

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|h^{\alpha}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq&C\|\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}(T_{0})e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(4.46)

Then multiplying (
4.46
) by
\varepsilon^{3/2}e^{-\nu_{0}T_{0}/(2\varepsilon)}

with
s=T_{0}
and summing over
\alpha\in\{A,B\}
yields

\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{h}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+3}{2}}.

(4.47)

∎

4.2.
W_{x}^{1,\infty}
estimate

Verification of the key hypothesis (
4.20
) requires establishing
W_{x}^{1,\infty}
estimates for energy closure. Applying
D_{x}
to (
4.17
) gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\partial_{t}(D_{x}h^{\alpha})+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(D_{x}h^{\alpha})+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(D_{x}h^{\alpha})+\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}(D_{x}h^{\alpha})\\
&=-\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}-\frac{D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}h^{\alpha}-D_{x}\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}\Big]+\sum_{i=1}^{6}D_{x}(\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{i}),\end{split}

(4.48)

where
\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{i}
is described in (
4.18
). Along the trajectory, the solution
D_{x}h^{\alpha}
of the equation (
4.48
) is expressed as

\displaystyle D_{x}h^{\alpha}(t,x,v)=\,

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,0)\,(D_{x}h^{\alpha})(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)[\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha})\,h^{\alpha}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

(4.49)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{6}(D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{j})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.

First, the leading term in (
4.49
) satisfies the following bound:

\left|\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)D_{x}h^{\alpha}(0,\,X(0),\,v)\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Moreover, through direct computation, (
4.30
) immediately gives

|\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}|\lesssim 1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k}(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

which together with (
4.20
) implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)[\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}[1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k}(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Furthermore, (
4.1
) shows

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha}|\lesssim\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|B^{\alpha\beta}(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})}|d\sigma dv_{*}\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle.\end{split}

Next, it is easy to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha})\,h^{\alpha}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Now, let us focus on the fourth term in (
4.49
). First, applying Lemma (
4.7
), we see

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\mathcal{H}_{1}^{\alpha}|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle(\|\mathbf{h}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Consequently, we derive the bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{1})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}].\end{split}

Moreover, utilizing the exponential decay of
F_{i}
, and Lemma
2.5
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\mathcal{H}_{2}^{\alpha}|\lesssim\,\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{1}\,(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),\end{split}

which leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{2})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})].\end{split}

From (
4.30
), and the assumption (
4.20
), it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}|=&\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\Big(\Big|\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})h^{\alpha}\Big|+\Big|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})(D_{x}h^{\alpha})\Big|\Big)\\
\lesssim&\left\langle v\right\rangle(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),\\
|D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4}|=&\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\Big(\Big|\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi_{R})\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha})\Big|\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt+\Big|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}D_{x}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha})\Big|\Big)\\
\lesssim&(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\left\langle v\right\rangle(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Collecting these estimates yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}+D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\lesssim\,\,\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})].\end{split}

The bounds of
\mathbf{F}_{i}
in Lemma
2.5
rigorously proves

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}|\lesssim&\left\langle v\right\rangle\Big(\varepsilon^{k-1}\Big|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l-1}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})(D_{x}F^{\alpha}_{2k-1})}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&+\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}|\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi_{i})|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\frac{\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+|\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\frac{\nabla_{v}(D_{x}F_{j}^{\alpha})}{\left\langle v\right\rangle}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\\
\lesssim&\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{2},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6}|\lesssim&\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}\left\langle v\right\rangle\Big(\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}D_{x}\mathbf{F}_{i}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\,\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}\mathbf{F}_{j}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt+\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\mathbf{F}_{i}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\,\Big\|\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}D_{x}\mathbf{F}_{j}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\\
\lesssim&\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

Then, it follows that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}+D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

Next, recalling the expressions in Lemma
4.1
that
