\displaystyle\begin{split}&D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})=(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{h}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{x}\mathbf{h}),\end{split}

with corresponding estimates

\displaystyle\begin{split}|K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{x}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))|&\lesssim{\delta}^{3+1}\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle e^{-c|V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{\mathbf{h}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},\\
|(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))|&\lesssim{\delta}^{3+1}\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle e^{-c|V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}}\|\mathbf{\mathbf{h}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},\end{split}

which implies

\displaystyle|D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))|\lesssim{\delta}^{3+1}\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle e^{-c|V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}}(\|\mathbf{\mathbf{h}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{\mathbf{h}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}).

Hence, for hard sphere collisions, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\,\delta^{3+1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})].

Finally, let us turn to the last term in (
4.49
). Noticing that

D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h})=(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{h}),

which together with Lemma
4.3
and (
4.21
) yields

\displaystyle(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})h^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*},

(4.50)

and

\displaystyle K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})(D_{x}h^{\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.

(4.51)

Define the integral operators

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=I_{1x}.\\
&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=I_{2x},\end{split}

then the estimate for
I_{1x}
follows by substituting (
4.36
) into (
4.50
)

I_{1x}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{2}\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{i,x}(t)+\mathcal{O}_{x}^{\alpha}(t).

(4.52)

The proof of Lemma
4.3
establishes that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{0,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,0)h^{\beta}(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{1,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 128.0374pt\times\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j}(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}(\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+({1+\mathcal{I}_{1}})\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\mathcal{I}_{2}\varepsilon^{k},

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{2,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\delta^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Moreover, the last term in (
4.52
) is expressed by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{O}_{x}^{\alpha}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta,c}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds.\end{split}

Following the methodology established in Cases 1-3b (Section 4.1) and applying Lemma
4.3
for
D_{x}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}

estimates, we obtain

\begin{split}\mathcal{O}_{x}^{\alpha}(t)\leq&\frac{C}{N}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+C\lambda e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3/2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Consequently, the bound for
I_{1x}
can be established as

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1x}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\varepsilon^{4}]e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

The analysis of
I_{2x}
begins by substituting
v\to v_{*}
in (
4.49
) and inserting into (
4.51
), which combined with prior estimates gives

I_{2x}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{3}\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{i,x}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathcal{D}_{i,x}^{\alpha}(t),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{0,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,0)D_{x}h^{\beta}(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{1,x}(t)=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})[\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\alpha}+\frac{D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}](s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{2,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})D_{x}\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j}(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+\mathcal{I}_{2}\varepsilon^{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 36.98866pt+({1+\mathcal{I}_{1}})\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{3,x}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 34.14322pt\lesssim\delta^{3+1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

By applying Lemma
4.3
and adapting the methodology from Section 4.1, one obtains

\displaystyle\begin{split}|\mathcal{D}_{1,x}^{\alpha}(t)|\leq(\frac{C}{N}+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}+C\lambda)\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3/2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

The estimation of
\mathcal{D}_{2,x}^{\alpha}(t)
follows the
L^{\infty}
framework in Section 4.1 until the final conversion to
L^{2}
norm. Beginning from this critical transition, consider Case 3b with velocity truncations:

|v|\leq N,|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N,
and
s-s_{1}\geq\varepsilon\lambda

where
\lambda
is sufficiently small. The differential structure yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{|v^{*}|\leq 2N,|v^{**}|\leq 3N}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})(D_{x}\mathbf{h})(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})ds_{1}dv_{**}dv_{*}ds.\end{split}

Moreover, noting that
\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}
remains constant, the variable substitution from
v_{*}
to

\zeta_{\alpha\beta}=X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})=X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),v_{*}),

such that

|\zeta_{\beta\alpha}-X^{\alpha}(s)|=|X^{\beta,\alpha}_{1}(s_{1})-X^{\alpha}(s)|\leq C(s-s_{1}).

Applying Lemma
4.8
to
X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})=X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),v_{*})
, with

x=X^{\alpha}(s;t,x,v),\quad\tau=s_{1},\quad t=s,

and integrating by parts, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})(D_{x}k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

(4.53)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

(4.54)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}(D_{x}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}})(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})D_{x}(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}(D_{x}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1})(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

(4.55)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),

where

D=\{|v_{*}|\leq 2N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N\}
,
\hat{D}=\{|\zeta-X(s)|\leq C(s-s_{1})N,|v_{**}|\leq 3N\}
.

Then, following
the same argument of
L^{\infty}
bounds in Section 4.1, (
4.53
),(
4.54
) and (
4.55
) are bounded by
\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
. Next we estimate
|D_{x}(\frac{dv_{*}}{\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})|
to bound the other part. Noting that

D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})]=D_{x}[\frac{1}{\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})}]=-\frac{1}{[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})]^{2}}D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})].

Lemma
4.8
implies

\frac{1}{4(s-s_{1})^{6}}\leq\frac{1}{\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})}\leq\frac{4}{(s-s_{1})^{6}}.

Let us set

X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})=\Big(X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,1}(s_{1}),X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,2}(s_{1}),X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,3}(s_{1})\Big),\quad v_{*}=(v_{*1},v_{*2},v_{*3}),

then the derivative matrix can be specifically represented as

(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})=\begin{pmatrix}&\partial_{v_{*1}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,1}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*2}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,1}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*3}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,1}(s_{1})\\
&\partial_{v_{*1}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,2}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*2}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,2}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*3}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,2}(s_{1})\\
&\partial_{v_{*1}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,3}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*2}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,3}(s_{1})&\partial_{v_{*3}}X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1,3}(s_{1})\end{pmatrix}.

By (
4.21
), the determinant is bounded by

|D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})]|\lesssim|\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})|^{2}\,|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})|\lesssim(s-s_{1})^{2}|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})|.

Since
s_{1}-s\geq\lambda\varepsilon
, it yields that

D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})]=-\frac{1}{[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})]^{2}}D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})]\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})|}{(s-s_{1})^{4}}\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})|}{(\lambda\varepsilon)^{4}}.

From the above analysis, we conclude that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|D_{x}[\text{det}(\frac{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}}{dv_{*}})]\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\hat{D})}\leq\frac{C_{\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\hat{D})}\\
&=\frac{C_{\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\int_{\hat{D}}|\partial_{x}\partial_{v_{*}}X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,z,v_{*})|^{2}\text{det}\Big(\frac{\partial X^{\beta}(s_{1};s,X^{\alpha}(s,x,v),v_{*})}{\partial X^{\alpha}(s,x,v)}\Big)dzdv_{**}dv_{*}ds_{1}ds\\
&\leq\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}.\end{split}

Thus, the main integral admits the bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})D_{x}(\frac{dv_{*}}{\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds\Big|\\
&\leq\frac{C_{\lambda,T_{0},N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Consequently, it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{2,x}^{\alpha}(t)\leq&(\frac{C}{N}+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}+C\lambda)\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

In addition,
I_{2x}
admits the following bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2x}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{4}]e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{C_{\lambda,T_{0},N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

For fixed
\lambda>0
, selecting
N>0
sufficiently large, we show that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C(\|\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle+(\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\lambda+C\varepsilon+\delta^{4})\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

(4.56)

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+C\varepsilon^{k-1}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}.

Remark 4.10
.

From the above estimate, it can be seen that the process of transforming
L^{\infty}

into
L^{2}
through integration by parts will generate the term
\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}e^{\frac{\nu_{0}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}
. Due to the coefficient
C_{\lambda,N}
in front of it, we need an additional
\varepsilon
to multiply with it in order to make it smaller. Hence, we require
\varepsilon^{5}
. Therefore, the index
k
of the expansion term must be greater than or equal to
6
.

We note that (
4.56
) contains the term
C\varepsilon\displaystyle{\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})
. As a result, we need to estimate
\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
to close the energy.

4.3.
W_{v}^{1,\infty}
estimate

Taking

D_{v}
on both sides of (
4.17
), one obtains

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\partial_{t}(D_{v}h^{\alpha})+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(D_{v}h^{\alpha})+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(D_{v}h^{\alpha})+\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}(D_{v}h^{\alpha})\\
&=-D_{x}h^{\alpha}-\frac{D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}h^{\alpha}-D_{v}[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}]+\sum_{i=1}^{6}D_{v},(\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{i}),\end{split}

(4.57)

where
\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{i}
is described in (
4.18
). Along the trajectory, the solution
D_{v}h^{\alpha}
of the equation (
4.57
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle D_{v}h^{\alpha}(t,x,v)=\,

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,0)\,(D_{v}h^{\alpha})(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}h^{\alpha})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha})\,h^{\alpha}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{6}(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{j})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

(4.58)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.

As in the spatial derivative case, the first line and the second line in (
4.58
) can be estimated

\left|\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)(D_{v}h^{\alpha})(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}h^{\alpha})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}h^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Moreover, from the expression in (
4.1
) for hard sphere case (
\gamma=1
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha}|\leq&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|D_{v}B^{\alpha\beta}|(|v-v_{*}|,\cos\theta)\mu^{\beta}(v_{*})d\sigma dv_{*}\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle,\end{split}

then the third line in (
4.58
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha})h^{\alpha}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|h^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Now, let us turn to the fourth line in (
4.58
). First, from lemma
4.7
, one gets

\sum_{\beta=A,B}\Big|D_{v}\Big[\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}Q^{\alpha\beta}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\alpha},\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}h^{\beta})\Big]\Big|\lesssim\,\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),

which implies

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{1})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\{(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\}.

Next, by the exponential decay property of
F_{i}
, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{v}\mathcal{H}_{2}^{\alpha}|\lesssim\,\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{1}\,(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{2})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Since
D_{v}[\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})]\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}
, it satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}|\lesssim\left\langle v\right\rangle(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

By (
2.26
) and the assumption (
4.20
), one gets

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4}|\lesssim(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\left\langle v\right\rangle(\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}+D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{4})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\,\,\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Finally, Lemma
2.5
provides the estimate of

\mathbf{F}_{i}
, then we have

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt|D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}|+|D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6}|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathcal{I}_{2},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{5}+D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{6})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.

For the singular part in the fifth line, we first split

(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})=(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{h}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{v}\mathbf{h}).

Employing Lemma
4.1
(
\gamma=1
), one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}|K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}(D_{v}\mathbf{h})(v)|&\lesssim\,{\delta}^{3+1}\left\langle v\right\rangle e^{-c|v|^{2}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\\
|(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi})\mathbf{h}(v)|&\lesssim\,{\delta}^{2+1}\left\langle v\right\rangle e^{-c|v|^{2}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Under hard sphere conditions, one obtains

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\,\delta^{3}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Finally, the analysis now concentrates on the sixth line, where the regular part admits the decomposition

D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h})=(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{h}),

which satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})h^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*},\end{split}

(4.59)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})(D_{v}h^{\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.\end{split}

(4.60)

We introduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=I_{1v},\\
&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=I_{2v}.\end{split}

Then, substituting (
4.36
) into (
4.59
), leads to

I_{1v}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{2}\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{i,v}(t)+\mathcal{O}_{v}^{\alpha}(t).

(4.61)

The expression (
4.11
) implies

|(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))|\leq C\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle^{\gamma}|\left\langle v_{*}\right\rangle\mathbf{h}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}.

Note that

\displaystyle|K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{h}(v)|+|(D_{x}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{h}(v)|\leq C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma-1}|\mathbf{h}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},

which means that
D_{v}
introduces an additional polynomial weight
\left\langle v_{*}\right\rangle
to the operator
K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}
. For
|v_{*}|\leq 1
and
T_{0}
small enough, it holds that

\sup_{s\in[0,T_{0}]}|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})|\leq C.

When
|v_{*}|>1
, for
0\leq s\leq t\leq T_{0}
, we have

\frac{|v_{*}|}{2}\leq|V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})|\leq 2|v_{*}|.

The conversion from
v_{*}
to
V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})
leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{0,v}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 54.06006pt\times\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,0)h^{\beta}(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))dv_{*}ds\\
\lesssim&\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},\\
\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{1,v}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j}(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
\lesssim&({1+\mathcal{I}_{1}})\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\mathcal{I}_{2}\varepsilon^{k}\\
&\hskip 76.82234pt+\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}].\end{split}

The equation (
4.8
) further reveals

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{V^{\alpha}(s)}{v_{*}}\frac{w_{\gamma}(V^{\alpha}(s))}{w_{\gamma}(v_{*})}|k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})|\leq C|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}\,\,e^{-c(|V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}+|v_{*}|^{2})},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\frac{V^{\alpha}(s)}{v_{*}}\frac{w_{\gamma}(V^{\alpha}(s))}{w_{\gamma}(v_{*})}|k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta}(V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\frac{(1+|V^{\alpha}(s)|+|v_{*}|)^{\gamma-1}}{|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|}\,e^{-c(|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|^{2}+\frac{||V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}-|v_{*}|^{2}|^{2}}{|V^{\alpha}(s)-v_{*}|^{2}})}.

Through conversion chain
v_{*}\to V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1})\to v_{**}
and (
4.4
), the final term satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{J}^{\alpha}_{2,v}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\,\delta^{2+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

The last term in (
4.61
) is expressed by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{O}_{v}^{\alpha}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta,c}\mathbf{h})(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
\leq&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|D_{v}(k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|h^{\beta^{\prime}}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})dv_{**}ds_{1}dv_{*}ds.\end{split}

Building upon the transformation from
v_{*}
to
v_{**}
, Cases 1-3b in Section 4.1 are reapplied with Lemma
4.3
’s estimates for
D_{v}(k_{M,\langle v\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta})
, that is

\begin{split}|\mathcal{O}_{v}^{\alpha}(t)|\leq&\frac{C}{N}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+C\lambda e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3/2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Hence, the
I_{1v}
bound emerges as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1v}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{2+1}]e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

Next, we turn to
I_{2v}
, and
substitute (
4.58
) into (
4.60
) to obtain

I_{2v}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{h})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{3}\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{i,v}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathcal{D}_{i,v}^{\alpha}(t),

(4.62)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{0,v}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,0)(D_{v}h^{\beta})(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}^{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{1,v}(t)=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})(D_{x}h^{\alpha}+\frac{D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}h^{\alpha})(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}h^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|h^{\alpha}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{2,v}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\sum_{j=1}^{6}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})(D_{v}\mathcal{H}^{\beta}_{j})(s_{1},X_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}),V_{1}^{\alpha,\beta}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+\mathcal{I}_{2}\varepsilon^{k}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+({1+\mathcal{I}_{1}})\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})],

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}^{\alpha}_{3,v}(t)=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,w}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt\times\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{h})(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\lesssim\delta^{3+1}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Employing Lemma
4.3
’s estimates for
D_{v}k_{M,\langle v\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}
, similar procedures yield

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{1,v}^{\alpha}(t)=&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{F}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times(D_{v}K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\beta,c})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}dv_{*}ds\\
\leq&(\frac{C}{N}+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}+C\lambda)\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3/2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

(4.63)

For Case 3b (
|v|\leq N
,
|v_{*}|\leq 2N
,
|v_{**}|\leq 3N
,
s-s_{1}\geq\varepsilon\lambda
), the variable change
v_{*}\to\zeta_{\alpha\beta}
with integration by parts gives:

\displaystyle\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})D_{v}\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\int_{D}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{0}^{s-\varepsilon\lambda}\mathcal{F}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})k_{M,N}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\,

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{\beta^{\prime}=A,B}(D_{v}k_{M,N}^{\beta\beta^{\prime}})(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})\mathbf{h}(s_{1},\zeta_{\alpha\beta},v_{**})(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})ds_{1}dv_{**}d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}ds

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.

where
(\frac{dv_{*}}{d\zeta_{\alpha\beta}})
is independent of
v_{**}
, and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{2,v}^{\alpha}(t)\leq&(\frac{C}{N}+C_{\eta}\,e^{-\frac{\eta}{8}N^{2}}+C\lambda)\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle I_{2v}

\displaystyle\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+(C\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{3})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x,v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}

(4.64)

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}].

Then, we obtain the
W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}
estimate as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}&\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+(\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\lambda+C\varepsilon+\delta^{3})\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+\varepsilon^{k-1}e^{\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

(4.65)

Multiplying (
4.65
) by
\varepsilon^{5}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}
leads to:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq&\frac{1}{4}(\varepsilon^{5}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+C(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+1}).\end{split}

(4.66)

Analogous to the
\displaystyle{\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{h}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}
estimate, we obtain

\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+3}{2}}.

These estimates collectively establish that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
\leq&\,\frac{1}{2}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}).\end{split}

Then, for time
t
satisfies
T_{0}<t\leq T_{L}=\varepsilon^{-\frac{2k-3}{2(2k-1)}}
, there exists a positive integer
n_{t}
and
0\leq t_{s}<T_{0}
, such that

t=n_{t}T_{0}+t_{s}
. Then we can deduce that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}((n-1)T_{0}+t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}((n-1)T_{0}+t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle+C(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{1}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{4}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}((n-2)T_{0}+t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}((n-2)T_{0}+t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle+(C+\frac{C}{2})(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{1}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,...

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{2^{n}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}(t_{s})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

(4.67)

\displaystyle+(C+\frac{C}{2}+...+\frac{C}{2^{n}})(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{1}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}.

Consequently, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
\lesssim&\,\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(4.68)

By the same iterative process, we also obtain that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{h}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

(4.69)

Remark 4.11
.

The estimate (
4.68
) reveals that for hard sphere interactions
(\gamma=1)
, the initial data incurs a polynomial weight loss of
\langle v\rangle^{2-1}
. Subsequent analysis in Section 5 demonstrates this generalizes to
\langle v\rangle^{2-\gamma}
for
-3<\gamma<1
, originating from the
D_{v}(\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3})
term.

4.4.
The proof of Theorem
1.1

[hard sphere part] Plugging (
4.68
) and (
4.69
) into (
3.4
), we derive that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2})+\frac{c_{0}}{2\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathcal{P})\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\leq C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\Big(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+1}{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-3}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{{L^{2}_{x}}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt+C\Big[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}\Big](\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})+C\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}.

(4.70)

Moreover, applying the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-3}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{k-2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{{L^{2}_{x}}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}}+1,\\
\end{split}

with the subsequent estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&C\Big[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}\Big](\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}})+C\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}\\
\lesssim&\,\Big[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\Big](\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x}}}+1).\end{split}

Due to
\frac{1}{C}<\theta<C
, it is easy to see

\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\lesssim\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|^{2}_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}\lesssim\displaystyle{\sum_{\alpha=A,B}}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.

(4.71)

Consequently, (
4.70
) reduces to

\begin{split}&\hskip 28.45274pt\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\\
\leq&C\sqrt{\varepsilon}\Big(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+1}{2}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\\
&\,\,\,+C\Big[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\Big]\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big).\end{split}

Combining with
\int_{0}^{t}(1+s)^{-q}ds<\infty
leads to

\begin{split}&\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\leq\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}(0)|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\\
&\,\,\,\times\exp\Big\{\int_{0}^{t}C\Big[\sqrt{\varepsilon}(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}})+\frac{1}{(1+t)^{q}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}\Big]ds\Big\}\\
&\leq\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}(0)|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\\
&\,\,\times\exp[C+Ct\sqrt{\varepsilon}(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}})+Ct(\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\mathcal{I}_{2})].\end{split}

Then for the time scale
0\leq t\leq T_{L}=\varepsilon^{-m}
(
0<m\leq\frac{2k-3}{4k-4}
), the integrals satisfy

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{I}_{1}\leq 2\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}(1+t)^{i-1}\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{1-m})^{i-1}\leq C,\\
&\mathcal{I}_{2}=\sum_{2k\leq i+j\leq 4k-2}\varepsilon^{i+j-2k}(1+t)^{i+j-2}\leq C(1+\varepsilon^{-m})^{2k-2}\leq C\varepsilon^{-m(2k-2)},\end{split}

(4.72)

implying the bound

\mathcal{I}_{1}t\varepsilon+\mathcal{I}_{2}t\varepsilon^{k-1}\leq C(\varepsilon^{1-m}+\varepsilon^{k-1-m(2k-1)})\leq C\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-m}.

(4.73)

For sufficiently small
\varepsilon
, this establishes

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\Big(\sum_{\alpha=A,B}\|\sqrt{\theta}f^{\alpha}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}(0)|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+1\Big)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\Big(1+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-m}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}-m}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big).\end{split}

The norm equivalence (
4.71
) consequently gives

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}(\|\mathbf{f}_{R}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2})\lesssim\,1+\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{\phi}_{R}(0)|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{h}^{\rm in}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}.

(4.74)

Therefore, combined with (
4.68
) and (
4.74
), this completes the proof for hard sphere case in Theorem
1.1
.

Remark 4.12
.

The estimate (
4.73
) indicates the validity time scaling as
O(\varepsilon^{-\frac{2k-3}{2k-1}})
, determined by the coefficients of the truncated expansion’s remainder terms. Considering the ansatz

F_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}=\sum_{i=0}^{n}\varepsilon^{i}F^{\alpha}_{i}+\varepsilon^{r}F_{R}^{\alpha},\quad n\geq r\geq 1,

(4.75)

with time scale
T_{L}=\varepsilon^{-m}

(0<m<1)
, substitution into (
1.6
) yields through comparison with (
4.72
) and (
4.73
):

\displaystyle\begin{split}t\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}&\leq Ct\varepsilon\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{1-m})^{i-1}\leq C\varepsilon^{1-m},\\
t\varepsilon^{r-1}\mathcal{I}_{2}&\leq C(1+\varepsilon^{n-r-m(n-1)}),\end{split}

requiring the constraint
0<m<\frac{n-r}{n-1}
. The case
n=2k-1
,
r=k
corresponds to
0<m<\frac{1}{2}
.

5.
The
W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}
estimate of the remainder term for potential range
-1\leq\gamma<1

Before presenting the proof, the key difficulty for
\gamma\neq 1
cases is outlined. From (
4.29
) and (
4.30
), the following estimates emerge:

•

\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}=-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}})h^{\alpha}_{R}
,
|\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}|\thicksim\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\mathbf{h}_{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
,

•

\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\mathcal{H}^{\alpha}_{3}(s,X(s),V(s))ds\right|\lesssim\varepsilon(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\displaystyle{\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{h}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})
.

For
-3<\gamma<1
, the preceding analysis shows this term cannot be absorbed by the left-hand side expression. The term fundamentally belongs to the linear part, though under the hard sphere model (
\gamma=1
), the dominant influence of
\mathcal{F}_{0}^{\alpha}
permits its treatment as a nonlinear term. For
\gamma<1
cases, inclusion in the linear part becomes necessary for proper analysis. This motivates the introduction of a new weight function
w(t,x,v)
for the remainder terms, leading to consideration of the linear part operator:

\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{w(t,x,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{w(t,x,v)})-\frac{(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})w(t,x,v)}{w(t,x,v)}.

(5.1)

The second term in (
5.1
) exhibits
\langle v\rangle=(1+|v|)
velocity growth, presenting two key challenges for the linear component analysis:

•

Indefinite sign behavior complicating direct estimates,

•

Stronger velocity growth compared to the first term.

This necessitates the equation transformations to generate dominant negative terms with accelerated velocity decay. While the weight functions depending on spatial derivatives may introduce structural complications, a
(t,v)
-dependent weighting strategy offers distinct advantages. The natural exponential function
e^{x}
proves particularly suitable for this construction due to its differentiation properties:

\frac{d}{dt}(e^{g(t,v)})=e^{g(t,v)}\cdot\frac{d}{dt}g(t,v).

To ensure that
g(t,v)
yields a sufficiently strong polynomial decay in
v
for controlling
\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{3}
, we impose the following conditions on parameters
\kappa_{0},\kappa_{1}>0
and
\kappa_{2}\in(1,2]

\omega_{\kappa}=\exp\{{\kappa_{0}[1+(1+t)^{-\kappa_{1}}]\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}\},\,\,\quad\text{and}\quad\,\,\,S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}=\frac{\omega_{\kappa}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}F_{R}^{\alpha}.

Then the remainder equation transforms to:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{t}S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}&+v\cdot\nabla_{x}S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}+\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\kappa}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\kappa}})S^{\alpha}_{R,\kappa}-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\kappa}}{\omega_{\kappa}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{j}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\omega_{\kappa}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\omega_{\kappa}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{R,\kappa},\end{split}

(5.2)

and the main linear part in (
5.2
) satisfies

\displaystyle\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(t,x,v):

\displaystyle=\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)})-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\Big(\frac{1}{2\theta_{M}}+2\kappa_{0}\Big)\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}}

(5.3)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{C\varepsilon}-C\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}}{C}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}}.

For parameter
0<\iota<1
, Young’s inequality gives

\begin{split}\left\langle v\right\rangle&=\Big(\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\varepsilon^{\iota-1}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\Big)^{\frac{\kappa_{2}-1}{\kappa_{2}-\gamma}}\Big(\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}\varepsilon^{\frac{(1-\iota)(\kappa_{2}-1)}{1-\gamma}}(1+t)^{-(1+\kappa_{1})}\Big)^{\frac{1-\gamma}{\kappa_{2}-\gamma}}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}\varepsilon^{\frac{(1-\iota)(\kappa_{2}-1)}{1-\gamma}}(1+t)^{-(1+\kappa_{1})}.\end{split}

(5.4)

Plunging (
5.4
) into (
5.3
), it holds that

\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}\geq(\frac{1}{C}-C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}+(\frac{\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}}{C}-C\varepsilon^{\frac{(1-\iota)(\kappa_{2}-1)}{1-\gamma}})\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}}.

Due to
0<\kappa_{0}\ll 1
, we need to find suitable
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
, such that

\frac{1}{C}-C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}>\frac{2}{3C}.

(5.5)

In fact, for
0<t\leq\varepsilon^{-m}
,
0<m\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{2k-3}{2k-1}
and
k\geq 6
, we have

C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2C\varepsilon^{\iota-\frac{m(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Then we can choose
1+\kappa_{1}=\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}
,

\frac{1-\gamma}{\kappa_{2}-1}=2,\,\,\,
i.e.

\,\kappa_{1}=\frac{2}{2k-1}
,
\kappa_{2}=\frac{3-\gamma}{2}
.
Then for
-1\leq\gamma<1
, it yields

\frac{m(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}\leq\frac{(2k-3)(2k+1)}{(2k-1)^{2}}.

Setting
\iota=\frac{(2k-2)(2k+2)}{4k^{2}}<1
gives

\iota-\frac{m(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}\geq\frac{(2k-2)(2k+2)}{4k^{2}}-\frac{(2k-3)(2k+1)}{(2k-1)^{2}}>\frac{1}{16k^{4}}>0.

For sufficiently small
\varepsilon

C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2C\varepsilon^{\iota-\frac{m(1+\kappa_{1})(1-\gamma)}{\kappa_{2}-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}<\frac{1}{3C},

which implies (
5.5
). Furthermore, with
\frac{(1-\iota)(\kappa_{2}-1)}{1-\gamma}>\frac{1}{4}
for
-1\leq\gamma<1
:

\Big(\frac{\kappa_{0}\kappa_{1}}{C}-C\varepsilon^{\frac{(1-\iota)(\kappa_{2}-1)}{1-\gamma}}\Big)\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}}>\frac{1}{4Ck}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\kappa_{2}}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}},\quad k\geq 6.

Finally, the weight function

\omega_{\gamma}:=\kappa_{0}\exp\Big[(1+(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{2k-1}})\langle v\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}\Big],\quad-1\leq\gamma<1,

(5.6)

leads to

\begin{split}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}:&=\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)})-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}\\
&\geq\frac{2\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{3C\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{4Ck}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}}{(1+t)^{\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}}>\frac{2\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{3C\varepsilon}.\end{split}

(5.7)

Remark 5.1
.

For the following estimates in this section, we actually replace
\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}

with
\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{C\varepsilon}
, where the coefficient
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}

in
\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{C\varepsilon}

is critical, as it can eliminate some singularities in these terms, such as
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\omega_{\kappa}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\omega_{\kappa}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{R,\kappa}

as well as the terms
\frac{D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}

and
\frac{D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}
. It should be emphasized that
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
cannot be replaced by any
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{l_{d}}}
, even if
\frac{10^{10}}{10^{10}+1}<l_{d}<1
, due to the mathematical structure that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{l_{d}}}\int_{s}^{t}\upsilon^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 8.5359pt\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{C\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}(t-s)}{\varepsilon^{l_{d}}}\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}ds\,\,\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{l_{d}}.

Remark 5.2
.

The weight function
\omega_{\gamma}:=\kappa_{0}\exp[(1+(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{2k-1}})\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}]

can help us eliminate the tricky term
\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{w(t,x,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{w(t,x,v)})
. However, the velocity weighting index
\frac{3-\gamma}{2}

cannot exceed 2, because of the joint equilibrium state
\mathbf{F}_{0}
, obtained from the conservation of momentum and energy. Otherwise, terms such as
\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}

(for
i=2,4,5
) cannot be controlled.

Mathematical Obstacles in Soft Potential Case

The term
\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}
lacks a positive lower bound as
v\to+\infty
, resulting in some mathematical difficulties.

Local Existence Analysis

We use
f(t,x,v)
to denote a nonlinear term.
The control of the weighted norm
\displaystyle{\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{|\gamma|}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
is particularly crucial for terms requiring
\varepsilon
-balancing with inherent
\frac{1}{\varepsilon}
coefficients.
We denote
the characteristic structure as

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)=:\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\right).

In hard potential case, it implies that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)f(t,x,v)ds\right|\lesssim\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)ds\right|\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

While in soft potential case, since
\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}
lacks a positive lower bound, one could only get

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)f(t,x,v)ds\right|\lesssim\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)ds\right|\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

To extract the small parameter
\varepsilon
, the following estimate is considered

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)f(t,x,v)ds\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\int_{0}^{t}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)ds\right|\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\frac{f(s)}{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{|\gamma|}f(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(5.8)

Long-Time Existence Analysis

For large time scales
t
, the solution estimates via temporal iterations (see (
4.67
)) are obstructed in soft potential cases by the absence of constant
\nu_{0}
satisfying (
4.26
). But if the initial
data of the remainder has a compact support, there is a hope
for solving this problem. We use
supp_{t}\{v|f(t,x,v)\}
to denote
the support of the function
f(t,x,v)
with respect to the variable

v
at time
t
. The radius of the support is defined as follows:

R_{s}(t|f(t,x,v))=\rm{sup}\{|v|:v\in\rm{supp}_{t}[f(t,x,v)]\}.

(5.9)

Given the finite initial support radius
R_{s}(0|S_{\gamma}(t,x,v))<+\infty
, the temporal evolution of the support radius requires careful analysis. Recall the characteristic equations

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{d}{d\tau}V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)),\quad V^{\alpha}(\tau;t,x,v)=v,\end{split}

By Lemma
4.8
and (
4.30
), it yields

\displaystyle R_{s}(t|f(t,x,v))\leq R_{s}(0|f(t,x,v))+Ct,

(5.10)

which imply bounded propagation speed for
R_{s}(t|f(t,x,v))
. Because of (
4.65
)-(
4.66
), the exponential decay term
e^{-\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}T_{0}/(2\varepsilon)}
must be small enough, which restricts the velocity domain to
v\sim O(\varepsilon^{-\iota_{d}/|\gamma|})
. Here the parameter
\iota_{d}
is selectable within
[\frac{10^{8}}{1+10^{8}},1)
, and for example, we can take

\iota_{d}:=\max\left\{\frac{10^{8}}{1+10^{8}},\frac{2k-3}{2k-1}\right\}.

Consequently, for all
t\in[0,T_{\varepsilon}]
with

T_{\varepsilon}\sim\begin{cases}&\varepsilon^{-\frac{2k-3}{2(2k-1)}}\,\,\,\,\qquad-1\leq\gamma\leq 1,\\
&\varepsilon^{-\frac{2k-3}{(1-\gamma)(2k-1)}}\quad-3<\gamma<-1.\end{cases}

In both cases by (
5.10
), it holds that:

\varepsilon\lesssim\varepsilon^{\iota_{d}}\lesssim|R_{s}(t|f)|^{|\gamma|}.

(5.11)

Therefore, we have

\frac{\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}\rightarrow+\infty,\quad e^{-\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}T_{0}/(2\varepsilon)}\rightarrow 0^{+},\quad as\quad\varepsilon\rightarrow 0^{+}.

which yields an iterable mathematical structure.

5.1.
L_{x,v}^{\infty}
estimates for
\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}
.

From the notation in (
5.7
) and
(
1.17
), the solution
S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}
of the
original nonlinear equation (
1.9
) can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{t}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}&+v\cdot\nabla_{x}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}+\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}\,S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}=\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma},\end{split}

(5.12)

where

\displaystyle\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(t,x,v)=\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)})-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)},

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{1}=\varepsilon^{k-1}\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}Q^{\alpha\beta}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma},\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}}S^{\beta}_{\gamma}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{2}^{\alpha}=\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}}S^{\beta}_{\gamma})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big],

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{3}=-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha}),

(5.13)

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{4}=-\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[\varepsilon^{k-1}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})F^{\alpha}_{2k-1}+\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}\Big],

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{5}=\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big\{\sum_{\beta=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k\atop 1\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k-1}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},F_{j}^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{j}^{\alpha},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big]\Big\}.

Making use of Duhamel’s principle, one has

\displaystyle\begin{split}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}(t,x,v)=&\exp\left(-\int_{0}^{t}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\int_{s}^{t}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\int_{s}^{t}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\int_{s}^{t}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.\end{split}

(5.14)

First, from (
5.7
), the inequality

\hat{\upsilon}_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}>\frac{\langle v\rangle^{\gamma}}{C\varepsilon}

holds, then we have

\exp\left(-\int_{0}^{t}\hat{\upsilon}_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right)<\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\langle V^{\alpha}(\tau)\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\right).

Moreover, setting

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{F}^{\alpha}_{0}}(t,s)

\displaystyle=:\exp\left(-\int_{s}^{t}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)

\displaystyle=:\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C\varepsilon}\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle V^{\alpha}(\tau)\right\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\right).

Then, (
5.14
) is estimated by

\displaystyle\begin{split}|S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(t,x,v)\leq&\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,0)|S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{5}|\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.\end{split}

(5.15)

Now let us control the terms on the right-hand side of (
5.15
). First, the estimates (
4.3
) and (
4.3
) hold for sufficiently small
T_{0}
. Consequently, when
0\leq\gamma<1
, there exists a constant
\nu_{o}>0
satisfying

\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\sim\nu_{o}\gtrsim 1.

Then for
0\leq s<t\leq T_{0}
, the following estimate holds:

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}s}{\varepsilon}}ds\right|\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}(t-s)}{C\varepsilon}\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}ds\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{\varepsilon}}.

For soft potentials with compactly supported initial data, the support propagation analysis (
5.10
) and (
5.11
) implies that when

-3<\gamma<0
, there exists an
\varepsilon
-dependent parameter
\nu_{\varepsilon}>0
such that

\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\sim\nu_{\varepsilon}>0.

(5.16)

This inequality holds for all
v>0
within the velocity support set and
t>0
in the maximal existence time interval
O(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{2k-3}{2k-1}})
, leading to

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\varepsilon}s}{\varepsilon}}ds\right|\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{\varepsilon}t}{\varepsilon}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp\left(-\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}(t-s)}{C\varepsilon}\right)\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}ds\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\varepsilon}t}{\varepsilon}}.

To streamline subsequent expressions, the following notation is introduced :

\nu_{\gamma}=\begin{cases}&\nu_{o}\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\,\quad 0\leq\gamma<1,\\
&\nu_{\varepsilon}\,\,\,\quad-3<\gamma<0.\end{cases}

Estimates for (
4.25
)’s right-hand side follow from

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}(0,\,X^{\alpha}(0),\,V^{\alpha}(0))\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}^{in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Next, we choose the weight function

\omega_{\gamma}=e^{\tilde{\kappa}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}}},\quad\tilde{\kappa}=\kappa_{0}(1+(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{2k-1}}),\quad-1\leq\gamma<1,

where

\kappa_{0}

is a sufficiently small constant. Substituting the weighting function

\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}
with
\omega_{\gamma}

preserves the estimation validity. Following the approach in (
4.28
)-(
4.32
), one obtains

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}_{1}^{\alpha}|=

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\left|\sum_{\beta=A,B}\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}}{\omega_{\gamma}},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{S^{\beta}_{\gamma}}{\omega_{\gamma}})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\,\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}_{2}^{\alpha}|=

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{\beta=A,B}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},\sqrt{\mu^{\beta}_{M}}\frac{S^{\beta}_{\gamma}}{\omega_{\gamma}})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}\frac{S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}}{\omega_{\gamma}},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\mathcal{I}_{1}\,\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{3}|=

\displaystyle|\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\mu^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i}F_{i}^{\alpha})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}},
