\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{4}|=

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[\varepsilon^{k-1}(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla_{x})F^{\alpha}_{2k-1}+\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k-1\atop 0\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k}\nabla_{x}\phi_{i}\cdot\nabla_{v}F_{j}^{\alpha}\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\mathcal{I}_{2},

\displaystyle|\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{5}|=

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{\beta=A,B}\mathop{\sum}_{i+j\geq 2k\atop 1\leq i,j\leq 2k-1}\varepsilon^{i+j-k-1}\frac{\omega_{\gamma}}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\Big[Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{i}^{\alpha},F_{j}^{\beta})+Q^{\alpha\beta}(F_{j}^{\alpha},F_{i}^{\beta})\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{k-1}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\mathcal{I}_{2}.

Consequently, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon(1+\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle\hskip 22.76228pt+\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.

Moreover, from Lemma
4.1
, one obtains

\displaystyle|K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))|\leq C{\delta}^{3+\gamma}\left\langle V^{\alpha}(s)\right\rangle^{\gamma}e^{-c|V^{\alpha}(s)|^{2}}|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}|_{L^{\infty}_{v}},

which leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\delta^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Finally, the regular part
K_{M,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}
is expressed by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,w}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[k_{M,1}^{\alpha\beta}+k_{M,2}^{\alpha\beta}](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\frac{w(V^{\alpha}(s))}{w(v_{*})}S_{\gamma}^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})S_{\gamma}^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.\end{split}

(5.17)

For clarity, we define the following operators

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{\mathcal{F}}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})=:\exp\left(-\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(\tau,X^{\alpha}(\tau),V^{\alpha}(\tau))d\tau\right),~~\mathcal{T}^{\beta}_{1}(s,s_{1})=:\exp\left(-\frac{1}{C\varepsilon}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\left\langle V^{\beta}(\tau)\right\rangle^{\gamma}d\tau\right).\end{split}

The bound for
S^{\beta}_{\gamma}(s,X(s),v_{*})
is derived as

\displaystyle\begin{split}|S^{\beta}_{\gamma}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})\leq&\mathcal{T}^{\beta}_{1}(s,0)|S^{\beta}_{\gamma}|(0,X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(0))\\
&+\sum_{j=1}^{5}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})|\mathcal{N}^{\beta}_{j}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})|K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1}\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{s}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{\beta}(s,s_{1})|K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}|(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}))ds_{1},\end{split}

(5.18)

which together with (
5.17
) yields

\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\leq\sum_{i=0}^{2}\hat{\mathcal{J}}_{i}(t)+\hat{\mathcal{O}}^{\alpha}(t).

(5.19)

Moreover, applying Lemma
4.3
with
-1\leq\gamma<1
, we obtain the kernel estimate:

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\beta\beta^{{}^{\prime}}}(s_{1},X^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),V^{\alpha,\beta}_{1}(s_{1}),v_{**})|dv_{**}\,dv_{*}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\leq\frac{C\left\langle V(s)\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{1+|V(s)|}\times\left\langle V_{1}(s_{1})\right\rangle^{\gamma}.\end{split}

The estimates in (
5.19
) follow from the same methodology developed in Section 4, that is, we can show

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}(s)&\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(1+\frac{s}{\varepsilon})e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}]\|\mathbf{S}^{in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}(T_{0})e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\\
&+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1}(T_{0}))\varepsilon+C\lambda+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+\delta^{3+\gamma}]\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},\end{split}

which leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq&C\|\mathbf{S}^{in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}(T_{0})e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(5.20)

For the hard potential case, the analysis directly follows from (
4.45
)-(
4.47
). In contrast, the soft potential case requires more careful consideration. Based on (
5.10
), (
5.11
) and (
5.16
), we observe the asymptotic behavior

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\rightarrow 0,\qquad as\quad\varepsilon\rightarrow 0.

(5.21)

Multiplying (
5.20
) by the scaling factor

\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}

, yields that

\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+3}{2}}.

(5.22)

5.2.
W_{x}^{1,\infty}
estimate

To confirm the crucial assumption (
4.20
), we have to
consider
W_{x}^{1,\infty}
estimates to close the energy. We take
D_{x}
of (
5.12
) to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\partial_{t}(D_{x}h_{R}^{\alpha})+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})+\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})\\
&=-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}-(D_{x}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}-D_{x}[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}]+\sum_{i=1}^{5}D_{x}(\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}),\end{split}

(5.23)

where
\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}
is described in (
5.13
). Along the trajectory, the solution
D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}
of the equation (
5.23
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}|D_{x}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(t,x,v)\leq\,&\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,0)\,|D_{x}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))\\
&+\frac{|e^{\alpha}|}{m^{\alpha}}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|(D_{x}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})\,S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{5}|D_{x}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{j}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\\
&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|D_{x}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.\end{split}

(5.24)

The first line in right hand side in (
5.24
) is bounded by

\left|\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)(D_{x}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma})(0,\,X(0),\,v)\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}^{in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

(5.25)

Following the approach in (
4.30
), the gradient estimates

|\nabla_{x}\phi_{R}|_{C^{1,\alpha_{0}}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}},\qquad|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|_{C^{1,\alpha_{0}}_{x}}\lesssim 1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}},

(5.26)

can be established. Under assumption (
4.20
), the second line of (
5.24
) can be controlled as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)[\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Moreover, the expression from (
5.13
)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(t,x,v)=\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)})-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)},\end{split}

leads to the term estimate

\displaystyle|(D_{x}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})\,S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(t,x,v)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|(D_{x}\upsilon^{\alpha})|}{\varepsilon}|S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|+|\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}(D_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})\cdot\frac{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\kappa}(t,v)})S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\Big[1+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon^{k}(\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 213.39566pt\times\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Then, the third line in (
5.24
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

Following the methodology in (
4.28
)-(
4.32
) (Section 4), the integral estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{5}(D_{x}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{1})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
\lesssim&\,\,\varepsilon(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{o}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]+\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}\\
&+\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}],\end{split}

is obtained. Lemma
4.1
yields the bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{x}(K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\,\delta^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

(5.27)

Finally, proceeding with the differentiation, we apply the product rule to obtain

D_{x}(K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})=(D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}+K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}),

whereupon we define the decomposed integrals by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=\hat{I}_{1x},\\
&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=\hat{I}_{2x}.\end{split}

The final line in (
5.24
) can be expressed in the form of an integral kernel that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{x}k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})S_{\gamma}^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*},\end{split}

(5.28)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})(D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.\end{split}

(5.29)

Substituting
v_{*}
for
v
in (
5.14
) and inserting into (
5.28
) yields

\hat{I}_{1x}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{2}\hat{\mathcal{J}}_{i,x}(t)+\hat{\mathcal{O}}_{x}^{\alpha}(t).

The expressions for
\hat{\mathcal{J}}_{i,x}
and
\hat{\mathcal{O}}_{x}^{\alpha}
mirror those in (
5.19
), with
K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}
replaced by
D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}
. Applying Lemma
4.3
and the methodology from Section 5.1 gives the estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{I}_{1x}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{3+\gamma}]e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

Analogously, replacing
v
with
v_{*}
in (
5.24
) and substituting into (
5.29
) produces

\hat{I}_{2x}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{x}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{3}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}^{\alpha}_{i,x}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\hat{\mathcal{D}}_{x}^{\alpha}(t).

(5.30)

The estimation procedure in (
4.52
) leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{I}_{2x}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+(C\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{3+\gamma})e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}].\end{split}

Therefore, the combination of (
5.25
)-(
5.27
) with the estimates for
\hat{I}_{x1}
and
\hat{I}_{x2}
establishes that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}&\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C(\|\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+(\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\lambda+C\varepsilon+\delta^{3+\gamma})\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+C\varepsilon^{k-1}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\\
&+\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{1-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})].\end{split}

(5.31)

5.3.
W_{v}^{1,\infty}
estimate

Applying the differential operator
D_{v}
to both sides of (
5.12
) yields:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\partial_{t}(D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})+v\cdot\nabla_{x}(D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})+\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}(D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha})\\
&=-D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}-(D_{v}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}-D_{v}[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}+K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}]+\sum_{i=1}^{5}D_{v},(\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}),\end{split}

(5.32)

where
\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i}
is described in (
5.13
). Along the trajectory, the solution
D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}
of the equation (
5.32
) is expressed as

\displaystyle|D_{v}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(t,x,v)\leq\,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,0)\,|D_{v}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}|(0,X^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v),V^{\alpha}(0;t,x,v))

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)\sum_{i=1}^{5}|D_{v}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{j}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

(5.33)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|(D_{v}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})\,S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)|D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})|(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds.

The first line in right hand side in (
5.33
) is bounded by

\left|\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,0)D_{v}S^{\alpha}_{\gamma}(0,\,X^{\alpha}(0),\,V^{\alpha}(0))\right|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}^{in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Following Subsection 4.2’s derivation, we estimate the second and third terms as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)[-D_{x}S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}+\sum_{i=1}^{5}(D_{v}\mathcal{N}^{\alpha}_{i})](s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon(1+\mathcal{I}_{1}+\varepsilon\mathcal{I}_{1})e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\\
&\hskip 22.76228pt+\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.\end{split}

(5.34)

Next, for
-1\leq\gamma<1
, recalling (
5.7
), we have

\begin{split}D_{v}(\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})&=\frac{D_{v}\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot D_{v}[\frac{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}(\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)})]-D_{v}[\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}{\omega_{\gamma}(t,v)}]\\
&\lesssim\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}+\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\frac{3-\gamma}{2}-1}}{(1+t)^{1+\kappa_{1}}}\lesssim\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2},\end{split}

where (
4.20
) has been used, then one gets

\displaystyle\begin{split}|(D_{x}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})\,S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}|(t,x,v)\leq&\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Hence the fourth line in (
5.33
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}})\,S_{\gamma}^{\alpha}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\,e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}),\end{split}

which together with Lemma
4.1
yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left|\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)D_{v}(K_{M,\left\langle v\right\rangle^{l}}^{\alpha,\chi}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds\right|\lesssim\,\,\delta^{2+\gamma}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{o}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).\end{split}

(5.35)

Finally, applying the product rule to the derivative operator yields

D_{v}(K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})=(D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}+K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}),

which naturally decomposes into two integral terms:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)(D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=\hat{I}_{1v},\\
&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}^{\alpha}_{0}(t,s)K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds:=\hat{I}_{2v},\end{split}

and the expression above can be rewritten as the form of integral kernels:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(D_{v}k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})S_{\gamma}^{\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*},\end{split}

(5.36)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}(D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))\\
=&\sum_{\beta=A,B}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha\beta}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})(D_{v}S_{\gamma}^{\beta})(s,X^{\alpha}(s),v_{*})dv_{*}.\end{split}

(5.37)

Plunging (
5.14
) into (
5.36
), we get

\hat{I}_{1v}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{2}\hat{\mathcal{J}}_{i,v}(t)+\hat{\mathcal{O}}_{v}^{\alpha}(t).

(5.38)

The operators
\hat{\mathcal{J}}_{i,v}
and
\hat{\mathcal{O}}_{v}^{\alpha}
maintain the form in (
5.19
) with the replacement:

K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c}\to D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c},

then it holds that

\displaystyle\hat{I}_{1v}

\displaystyle\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+[C(1+\mathcal{I}_{1})\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{3+\gamma}]e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}.

We perform the substitution
v\mapsto v_{*}
in (
5.33
) and insert it into (
5.37
), to obtain

\hat{I}_{2v}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{T}_{0}^{\alpha}(t,s)(D_{v}K_{M,\omega_{\gamma}}^{\alpha,c})\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(s,X^{\alpha}(s),V^{\alpha}(s))ds=\sum_{i=0}^{3}\hat{\mathcal{Z}}_{i,v}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\hat{\mathcal{D}}_{v}^{\alpha}(t).

(5.39)

Following the procedure from (
4.62
)–(
4.64
) gives

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2v}&\leq\,C\frac{t}{\varepsilon}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+(C\varepsilon+C{\lambda}+\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\delta^{2+\gamma})e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})]\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{N,\lambda,T_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{k}\mathcal{I}_{2}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt+C\varepsilon^{k}e^{-\frac{\nu_{\gamma}t}{\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}].\end{split}

Therefore, combining (
5.34
)–(
5.35
) with the estimates of
\hat{I}_{1v}
and
\hat{I}_{2v}
leads to:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}&\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+(\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\lambda+C\varepsilon+\delta^{\gamma+2})\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{3}}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|D_{v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+C\varepsilon^{k-1}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(5.40)

In summary, combining (
5.40
) with (
5.31
) establishes the following estimates for
W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}&\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\leq C(\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+(\frac{C_{\lambda}}{N}+C\lambda+C\varepsilon+\delta^{2+\gamma})\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{3}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+\frac{C_{\lambda,N}}{\varepsilon^{4}}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&+C\varepsilon^{k}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}[(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}+(e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}s}{2\varepsilon}}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})^{2}]+C\varepsilon^{k-1}e^{\frac{\nu_{\gamma}T_{0}}{2\varepsilon}}.\end{split}

(5.41)

Further, multiplying (
5.41
) by
\varepsilon^{5}
and applying (
5.21
) yields:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\Big[\varepsilon^{5}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\hskip 99.58464pt+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}(\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\Big]

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+1}.

Following the estimation procedure for
\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
in Section 5.1 gives:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+C\varepsilon^{\frac{2k+3}{2}}.

Consequently, the combined estimate becomes

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\leq\,\frac{1}{2}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})+C(\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{0}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}).\end{split}

For
t\in(T_{0},T_{S}]
, there exist positive integer
n_{t}
and
t_{s}\in[0,T_{0})
such that
t=n_{t}T_{0}+t_{s}
. Applying the iterative scheme analogous to (
4.67
) leads to:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{S}}(\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}_{\gamma}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\lesssim\,\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2-\gamma}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x,v}\mathbf{S}^{\rm in}_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{L}}\|\mathbf{f}_{R}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

6.
Shortening of the validity time for potential range
-3<\gamma<-1

This section considers the solution’s validity time for
-3<\gamma<-1
. The existence proof follows the methodology in Section 5. As established in (
1.14
), the weight function becomes
\gamma
-independent and takes the form

\omega_{-1}=e^{\tilde{\kappa}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}},\quad\tilde{\kappa}=\kappa_{0}[1+(1+t)^{-\frac{2}{2k-1}}],\quad 0<\kappa_{0}=\kappa_{0}(m^{A},m^{B})\ll 1.

Remark (
5.2
) indicates that the weight’s exponent
\frac{3-\gamma}{2}
cannot exceed 2, which makes it impossible to maintain the
O(\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{2k-3}{2k-1}})
validity time for
k\geq 6
. Substituting
\omega_{-1}=\exp(\tilde{\kappa}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}+\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\omega_{-1}(t,x)}{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}\nabla_{v}[\frac{\sqrt{\mu^{\alpha}_{M}}}{\omega_{-1}(t,x)}]-\frac{\partial_{t}\omega_{-1}(t,v)}{\omega_{-1}(t,v)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\upsilon^{\alpha}}{\varepsilon}-\frac{e^{\alpha}}{m^{\alpha}}(\frac{1}{2\theta_{M}}+2\kappa_{0})\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{2\kappa_{0}}{2k-1}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{C\varepsilon}-C\left\langle v\right\rangle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\frac{2\kappa_{0}}{C(2k-1)}\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}}.

For the parameter
0<\iota<1
, we apply Young’s inequality to obtain

\begin{split}\left\langle v\right\rangle&=[\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\varepsilon^{\iota-1}(1+t)^{\frac{(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}}]^{\frac{1}{2-\gamma}}[\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1-\iota}{1-\gamma}}(1+t)^{-\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}]^{\frac{1-\gamma}{2-\gamma}}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}}+\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1-\iota}{1-\gamma}}(1+t)^{-\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}.\end{split}

Consequently, we have

\hat{\upsilon_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}}\geq[\frac{1}{C}-C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}]\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{\gamma}}{\varepsilon}+[\frac{2\kappa_{0}}{C(2k-1)}-C\varepsilon^{\frac{1-\iota}{1-\gamma}}]\frac{\left\langle v\right\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\frac{2k+1}{2k-1}}}.

For sufficiently small
\varepsilon
, it follows that

\frac{2\kappa_{0}}{C(2k-1)}-C\varepsilon^{\frac{1-\iota}{1-\gamma}}\geq\frac{\kappa_{0}}{C(2k-1)}>0.

The validity time scales as
O(\varepsilon^{-m_{\gamma}})
for
-3<\gamma<-1
, abbreviated as
\varepsilon^{-m_{\gamma}}
, with the exponent satisfying
0\leq m_{\gamma}\leq\frac{2k-3}{2(2k-1)}
. This leads to the derivation

C\varepsilon^{\iota}(1+t)^{\frac{(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2C\varepsilon^{\iota-\frac{m_{\gamma}(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

For
\iota=\frac{(2k-2)(2k+2)}{4k^{2}}<1
and
m_{\gamma}=\frac{2k-3}{(1-\gamma)(2k-1)}
, a direct computation shows

\iota-\frac{m_{\gamma}(2k+1)(1-\gamma)}{2k-1}=\frac{(2k-2)(2k+2)}{4k^{2}}-\frac{(2k-3)(2k+1)}{(2k-1)^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{16k^{4}},\quad k\geq 6,

which implies

\displaystyle T

\displaystyle=O(\varepsilon^{-y}),\quad y=\begin{cases}\frac{2k-3}{2(2k-1)},&\gamma\in[-1,1],\\
\frac{2k-3}{(1-\gamma)(2k-1)},&\gamma\in(-3,-1),\end{cases}

for arbitrary
k\geq 6
in (
1.7
), thus establishing Theorem
1.1
.

Remark 6.1
.

When we have chosen the expansion form of (
1.7
), it can be seen from the above analysis that as
k
increases, the validity time of the solution is always monotonically increasing. Furthermore, as
k\rightarrow+\infty
, the validity time will approach the upper bound, as shown in Figure2 at the beginning of the article. In addition, the argument process in this article can be used to analyze other expansions similarly, such as

F_{\varepsilon}^{\alpha}=\sum_{i=0}^{n}\varepsilon^{i}F^{\alpha}_{i}+\varepsilon^{r}F_{R}^{\alpha},\quad n\geq r\geq 1.

(6.1)

The validity time may be different. However, once the ratio of
\frac{r}{n}
is fixed, the validity time is determined, which always increase monotonically as
k
increases.
